Detailed local search algorithm:
- Maintain a array length n positive sum 1.
- For a candidate, compute c = conv. M = max(c). Score=2nM. We want M.
- We can implement a move: choose active set A = indices with c within q (e.g., 5%) of M. Compute subgradient g=2*corr(1_A/|A|, a). Then update a_i ← a_i exp(-η (g_i - avg_g)). This should reduce mass at indices that are often paired in active sums. To avoid reducing sum active, avg. The direction is steepest for weighted sum of active coefficients (with all equal weights). If active is central only (k around n-1), g_i ≈2 a_{n-1-i}; update reduces a_i proportionally to mirror mass. This symmetrizes? If a_i high at mirror of high, reduce both? It might shrink high pairs.
- If active set all near-flat, g_i ~sum a over all k active near i? It may smooth? We'll see.

Need choose η. We can perform line search: For eta values [0.5,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01], compute new candidate, exact self objective. Accept best improvement. This is gradient-like. To avoid underflow, if min a <1e-15 maybe reset? But power law min ~1/(2n) sum=1 =>1/n=1e-4, not tiny. Good.

import numpy as np, time, math

def _pow2(m): ...
def _fft_conv(a): ...
def _fast_eval(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    a=np.clip(a,0,None)
    S=a.sum()
    if S<=1e-12: return inf
    b=a/S
    c=_fft_conv(b)
    M=float(np.max(c))
    return 2.0*len(b)*M
def _fast_eval_full(a, return_c=False): ... also M, idx
def _smooth_random(n, rng, maxfreq, sigma):
    w=np.zeros(n);? 
    # Using real white noise then fft filter:
    x=rng.normal(size=n)
    X=np.fft.rfft(x, n)
    freqs=np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
    cutoff = maxfreq/n? 
    X[mask]=X[mask]/(1+ (freq/cutoff)^2)  # Butterworth
    y=np.fft.irfft(X,n)
    y = y - y.mean() # zero mean? But for positive multiplier, mean not matter.
    return y / (np.std(y)+1e-15)
```

Let's write smooth_random efficient:
```
def smooth_log_field(n, maxfreq, rng):
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    x = x/(n-1)
    y = np.zeros(n)
    for k in range(1, maxfreq+1):
        amp = rng.normal(0, 1.0/math.sqrt(k))
        phase = rng.uniform(0, 2*pi)
        y += amp*np.cos(2*pi*k*x + phase)
    # normalize
    sd = y.std()
    if sd>1e-12: y/=sd
    return y
```
For n=16000, maxfreq=10, each loop computes cos; 10*16k=160k ops, fine. Could precompute x for each n? Top-level caching? We can compute per proposal, okay. To speed, precompute `x` for n and reuse? But if multiple n, store x,best? Fine. Need no lambdas.

Block proposal:
```
def block_proposal(a, rng):
    cand=a.copy()
    n=len(a)
    width = int(rng.integers(max(8,n//50), max(16,n//5)))
    start = int(rng.integers(0, n-width))
    factor = math.exp(rng.normal(0, 0.35))
    u = np.linspace(0,1,width)
    env = 0.5*(1+np.cos(np.pi*(u-1)))? peak 1 at center. 
    cand[start:start+width] *= 1 + (factor-1)*env
    cand = np.clip(cand,0,None); normalize
    return cand
```

Gradient proposal:
```
def gradient_proposal(a, local_score, rng):
    score,c = fast_eval_full(a, return_c=True)
    n=len(a); M=c.max()
    # choose top active
    # To avoid threshold all:
    margins = [0.0, 0.001,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1]
    margin = M * margins[rng.integers(len(margins))]
    thr = M - margin
    w = np.maximum(c - thr, 0)
    sw = w.sum()
    if sw ==0 or (w>0).sum() < max(1,n//1000):
       # top fraction
       q = np.quantile(c, 0.9)
       w = np.maximum(c-q,0)
       sw=w.sum()
    w/=sw
    grad = active_gradient(w, a)
    d=grad - grad.mean()
    scale=np.max(np.abs(d))
    if scale<1e-12: return None
    d/=scale
    for eta in [0.5,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005]:
       cand = a*np.exp(-eta*d)
       cand=np.clip(cand,0,None); S=cand.sum(); if S==0 break; cand/=S
       s=fast_score(cand)
       if s < local_score: return cand,best candidate (we can keep within function?)
```
But this calls fast_score multiple times; gradient proposal single iteration. To optimize, we could line search and return best found even if worse? Return candidate and score maybe. Outer can evaluate? Avoid double. Let gradient function return best candidate among etas (including original? no if none improve). But outer has local_score. We can just implement in loop.

Let's attempt to design a direct convex/reverse algorithm for finite sequence:
Minimize M. Since objective is homogeneous nonconvex, but we can use "quadratic programming by max coefficient active". Suppose we guess active set of coefficients where max=M. We want find a≥0 sum1 such that c_k=M for active k and c_k≤M. This is like optimize energy. Consider Lagrangian L=∑ λ_k(c_k-M) + μ(∑a-1). KKT: 2∑λ_k a_{k-i}+μ=0? Since c_k quadratic, derivative 2A a = μ? For nonconvex, stationary points have A a = constant over active. If λ all positive, this is linear system. Could solve for a from dual: For given active weights λ, gradient zero: g_i =∑λ_k a_{k-i}=constant. This is eigenvalue-like. Then adjust λ? Not simple.

Maybe use iterative spectral: start with weights λ over coefficients (initialize uniform), solve linear system A(λ) a = ones with a≥0? Since derivative ∑λ_k a_{k-i}. The matrix A(λ) is symmetric Toeplitz-like with entries λ_{i+j}. Need solve. Then compute resulting convex profile, update λ to maximize weighted max. This resembles trust region / Lanczos for box? Could be efficient.

Given nonnegative a, if we choose λ as weights integrated over max (e.g., softmax), the minimizer of G = ∑ λ_k c_k - ν∑a with entropy regularization? Without entropy, unbounded negative? c_k can be negative? For nonnegative, no. The quadratic form aᵀH(λ)a. If H indefinite, min zero. Not.

Could add entropy regularization: minimize ∑λ c_k - γ∑a_i log a_i. The optimum satisfies 2∑λ_k a_{k-i} + γ log a_i + const=0. This can be solved via fixed point: a_i = exp(- (2/γ)∑λ_k a_{k-i}) normalized. This is similar to gradient updates with step 2/γ. We can implement (our log update) with active weights. Choose many γ (step sizes). This is what we do.

Maybe a more global algorithm: Use Weiszfeld-like fixed point for minimizing max with entropy: For current active weights proportional to exp(β c), update a by fixed point. This might converge to locally optimal profile. We can implement with β increasing (active narrowing). This is analogous to adaptive iterative weighted least squares.

Algorithm "ADMM"? Define h(a)=max c. Approximate by w_k(a)=exp(β c_k)/sum. Then update a ← softmax(- (2/τ) cross(w,a)). Iterate. As β large, focuses on max. This is exactly multiplicative gradient with adaptive step. We can use.

Let's formulate to improve stability:
- Let a normalized sum 1.
- Compute c, M.
- weights w = softmax(β c) or active threshold. Normalize.
- Compute g = cross(w,a) as above.
- Find τ (step) such that new a? Use line search as before.
This is good.

Let's refine fast eval for speed and accuracy.

```
def _fft_conv_norm(a):
    n = a.size
    L = 1
    m = 2*n - 1
    while L < m: L <<=1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, L)[:m]
    return conv
```
For normalized a, sum=1, conv sum ~1. Numerical imaginary discarded. Could negative values ~1e-18; max if all negative? no. To be safe: `conv = np.maximum(conv, 0.0)`? If there is a true zero and negative tiny, max unaffected. But if global peak subtle and negative error makes max smaller? Positive peak unaffected. Clipping negative to 0 slightly. OK.

`_fast_score(a)`:
```
def _fast_score(a, return_conv=False, return_maybe=False):
   arr=np.asarray(a,float)
   arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,None)
   S=arr.sum()
   if S<=1e-12: score=np.inf
   else:
       b=arr/S
       c=_fft_conv_norm(b)
       c=np.maximum(c,0.0) # maybe
       M=float(np.max(c))
       score=2.0*b.size*M
   if return_conv: return score,c
   return score
```
Every eval allocates b and c. For proposals we already normalized; can reduce overhead by `_fast_score_normalized(b)` assumes sum1, nonnegative. Use in inner loop:

```

def _random_smooth_field(n, maxfreq, rng, x=None):

   if x is None: x=get layout

Proposal generation:
```
def _make_proposal(local, rng, amp):
   n=local.size
   kind=rng.integers(3)
   if kind==0:
       maxfreq = int(rng.integers(3,13))
       y=_random_smooth_field(n,maxfreq,rng)
       eta=amp*rng.uniform(0.3,1.0)
       cand=local*np.exp(eta*y)
   elif kind==1:
       cand=local.copy()
       # random block
       width=int(rng.integers(max(4,n//80), max(8,n//8)))
       start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width))
       factor=math.exp(rng.normal(0,0.6*amp+0.1))
       u=np.linspace(0,1,width)
       env=0.5*(1+np.cos(np.pi*(u-1.0))) # 0 endpoints,1 center? at u=1: cos0=1 ->1; at0 cos(-pi)=-1->0
       cand[start:start+width] *= 1.0 + (factor-1.0)*env
       # maybe second block reverse
   else:
       # coordinate swap/pair transfer or small random element? maybe global but with random spikes
       cand=local.copy()
       idx=int(rng.integers(n))
       frac=rng.uniform(0.01,0.2)
       mass=local[idx]*frac
       j=int(rng.integers(n))
       if j != idx:
          cand[idx]-=mass
          cand[j]+=mass
   cand=np.clip(cand, 1e-300, None) # keep positive; but if want zeros, clip 0? small epsilon can distort. If a zero beneficial? Maybe not. Use 0 but then later multiplicative bad. Use 0.0, but if all positive no issue. For coordinate, ensure cand[idx]>=0. Use 0.
   S=cand.sum()
   if S<=0: return local.copy()
   return cand/S
```
For coordinate, transfer from idx to j may move mass to high profile; accept only if improves.

Gradient function top-level:
```
def _gradient_candidate(local, rng):
   score,c = _fast_score_norm(local, True)
   n=local.size
   M=float(np.max(c))
   if not np.isfinite(M) or M<=0: return local, score
   # active margins
   margin_fracs = [0.0,0.0005,0.001,0.003,0.01,0.03,0.08,0.2]
   frac = margin_fracs[int(rng.integers(len(margin_fracs)))]
   thr=M*(1.0-frac)
   w=np.maximum(c-thr,0.0)
   sw=w.sum()
   if sw <=0 or np.sum(w>0)<max(3,n//1000):
       # top f
       frac_top = [0.5,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01][int(rng.integers(6))]
       if n < 1000: q=0.0
       else: q=float(np.quantile(c, 1.0-frac_top))
       w=np.maximum(c-q,0.0)
       sw=w.sum()
   if sw<=0: return local, score
   w = w/sw
   grad=_active_gradient(w, local)
   d=grad - np.mean(grad)
   scale=float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
   if scale<1e-14: return local, score
   d/=scale
   best_cand=local.copy(); best_s=score
   etas=[0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002]
   for eta in etas:
       cand=local*np.exp(-eta*d)
       cand=np.clip(cand,0.0,None)
       S=cand.sum()
       if S<=0: continue
       cand/=S
       s=_fast_score_norm(cand)
       if s<best_s:
           best_cand=cand; best_s=s
   return best_cand, best_s
```
But outer will evaluate candidate again if we return without score. We can return score too.

`_active_gradient(w,a)`:
```
def _active_gradient(w,a):
   n=a.size
   q=2*n-1
   L=1; m=n+q-1=3n-2? q+ n -1=3n-2
   while L<m: L<<=1
   B=w[::-1]
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
   fb=np.fft.rfft(B,L)
   conv=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,L)[:n+q-1]
   seg=conv[n-1:2*n-1]
   return 2.0*seg[::-1]
```
Need check length: n+q-1 = n+2n-1-1=3n-2. Indices 0..3n-3. seg start n-1, stop 2n-1 exclusive? `conv[n-1:2*n-1]` length n, includes indices n-1..2n-2, correct. q-1=2n-2. Good.
Some weights maybe zero. Convolution with length B q. If w length 2n-1. Good.

Annealing loop:
```
def refine_local(local, local_score, best_holder, deadline, rng, amp):
   local=local.copy(); local_score=local_score
   no_improve=0
   total_iter=0
   while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       total_iter+=1
       # propose
       if rng.random()<0.15:
          cand,s = _gradient_candidate(local,rng)
       else:
          cand = _make_proposal(local,rng,amp)
          s = _fast_score_norm(cand)
       if not finite: continue
       if s < local_score - 1e-12:
           local=cand; local_score=s; no_improve=0
           if s < best_holder[0]:
               best_holder[0]=s; best_holder[1]=cand.copy(); top update
           # reduce amp slightly
           amp = max(0.05, amp*0.98)
       else:
           no_improve+=1
           # exponential bettering
           if s < local_score + 0.005 and rng.random() < 0.1: # accept small worse?
               local=cand; local_score=s
           elif no_improve > 5000:
               # reheat: perturb local strongly and reset no
               y=_random_smooth_field(local.size, maxfreq=3, rng)
               local=local*np.exp(0.5*y)
               local = normalize; local_score=_fast_score_norm(local)
               no_improve=0
               amp=0.3
           else:
               amp = min(0.8, amp*1.01)
       if total_iter % 500 ==0: print progress
       # Maybe periodically evaluate best exact? no.
   return local, local_score
```
But if we accept small worse, local may drift; best_holder tracks. Good.

    key=n

c=c[:2*n-1]
# due to possible negative small
if c.size:
   c = np.maximum(c, 0.0) # maybe but if max negative? no

`_fast_score_norm`:
```
def _fast_score_norm(a, return_conv=False):
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.inf, None
    c=_fft_conv_norm(a)
    M=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    score=2.0*n*M
    if return_conv: return score,c
    return score
```
Maybe ensure finite: if not finite return inf.

`_make_power_law`:
```
def _make_power_law(n, offset=1.0, tilt=0.0, right_power=0.0,ymmetric=False):
   i=np.arange(n,float)
   a=np.power(i+offset, -0.5)
   if right_power !=0:
       a *= np.power(n-i, float(right_power)) # 1..n
   if tilt !=0:
       x=(i/(n-1) if n>1 else 0)
       a *= np.exp(float(tilt)*x)
   a=np.clip(a,0,None)
   S=a.sum()
   if S>0: a/=S
   return a
```
If offset small >0. If offset=0, inf; avoid. Use offset>=0.25.

`_random_smooth_field`:
```
def _random_smooth_field(n, maxfreq, rng, x=None):
    if x is None: _,x=_get_layout(n)
    y=np.zeros(n)
    kmax=max(1,int(maxfreq))
    for m in range(1,kmax+1):
       amp=rng.normal(0.0, 1.0/math.sqrt(m))
       phase=rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0*math.pi)
       y += amp * np.cos(2.0*math.pi*m*x + phase)
    # Add small random constant? no.
    y -= y.mean()
    sd=float(np.std(y))
    if sd > 1e-15: y /= sd
    return y
```
This uses `x` normalized 0..1.

def _make_proposal(a, rng, amp):
    n=a.size
    kind=rng.integers(0,3)
    if kind==0:
       mf=int(rng.integers(2, 9+int(amp*10))) maybe 2..15
       y=_random_smooth_field(n,mf,rng)
       eta=amp*rng.uniform(0.2,1.2)
       cand=a*np.exp(eta*y)
    elif kind==1:
       cand=a.copy()
       width=int(rng.integers(max(8,n//100), max(16,n//10)))
       start=int(rng.integers(0,max(1,n-width+1)))
       factor=math.exp(rng.normal(0.0, max(0.05, amp)))
       u=np.linspace(0,1,width)
       env=0.5*(1.0+np.cos(np.pi*(u-1.0)))
       contributions = 1.0 + (factor-1.0)*env
       contributions = np.maximum(contributions, 1e-8)
       cand[start:start+width] *= contributions
       # optional second block
       if rng.random()<0.3:
          width2=int(rng.integers(max(8,n//200), max(8,n//40)))
          ...
    else:
       cand=a.copy()
       idx=int(rng.integers(n))
       frac=float(rng.uniform(0.02,0.3))
       mass=a[idx]*frac
       if mass>0:
          j=int(rng.integers(n*2))
          if j>=n or a[j]<=1e-300? choose any
          cand[idx]-=mass
          cand[j]=(cand[j]+mass)
    cand=np.ascontiguousarray(cand,dtype=float)
    np.clip(cand,0,1000,out=cand)
    S=cand.sum()
    if S>0: cand/=S
    else: return a.copy()
    return cand
```

def _top_weights(c, frac):
   n=c.size
   k=max(1, int(frac*n))
   idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
   thr=float(c[idx].min())
   w=np.maximum(c-thr,0)
   return w
```

def _active_weights(c, rng):
   n=c.size
   M=float(c.max())
   if rng.random()<0.5:
       # value margin

   else:
       frac=...

Lowdim basis and objective:
```
def _make_basis(n, K):
    x=(np.arange(n,float)/(n-1)-0.5) if n>1 else np.zeros(n)
    vals=[]
    vals.append(x)
    vals.append(x*x-1.0/12.0)
    for m in range(1, max(1,K-2)+1):
       vals.append(np.cos(2*np.pi*m*(x+0.5))) # cos on [0,1]
       vals.append(np.sin(2*np.pi*m*(x+0.5)))
    # truncate to K
    while len(vals)<K: vals.append(np.zeros(n))
    basis=np.array(vals[:K], dtype=float)
    # normalize columns to unit std
    for j in range(K):
       sd=basis[j].std()
       if sd>1e-12: basis[j]/=sd
    return basis
```
But if K=8, [x, x^2, cos2, sin2, cos4, sin4, cos6, sin6]. Good. x+0.5 = i/(n-1). Use zero mean for x^2 centered. Fourier mean zero approx. If column has mean, not a problem but constant term may scale; we can subtract mean: `basis[j] -= basis[j].mean()`. Then normalize. Do that.

`_lowdim_objective`:
```
def _lowdim_objective(v):
    data=_LOWDIM
    if not data: return 1e100
    v=np.asarray(v,float)
    pen=0.0
    lim=1.0
    amax=float(np.max(np.abs(v))) if v.size else 0.0
    if amax>lim:
       v=np.clip(v,-lim,lim)
       pen=0.5*(amax-lim)*(amax-lim)
    a = data['base'] * np.exp(v @ data['basis'])
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return 1e100+pen
    np.clip(a,0,None,out=a)
    S=a.sum()
    if S<=1e-12: return 1e100+pen
    a/=S
    return _fast_score_norm(a)+pen
```
`v @ basis`: if v length matches. Good. If K=0? no.

Main search details:
```
def _local_search(local, local_score, top, deadline, rng, best_ref):
    amp=0.12
    no=0
    iters=0
    last_print=time.monotonic()
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       iters+=1
       r=rng.random()
       if r<0.15:
          cand,s = _gradient_candidate(local,rng)
       else:
          cand = _make_proposal(local,rng,amp)
          s = _fast_score_norm(cand)
       if not np.isfinite(s):
          cand=local; s=local_score
       if s < local_score - 1e-12:
          local=cand; local_score=s; no=0
          if s < best_ref[0]:
              best_ref[0]=s; best_ref[1]=local.copy()
              _update_top(top, s, local)
          amp=max(0.05, amp*0.985)
       else:
          no += 1
          # Sometimes accept if close
          if s < local_score + 0.002 and rng.random()<0.2:
              local=cand; local_score=s
          if no > 2000:
              # reheat current
              y=_random_smooth_field(local.size, maxfreq=int(rng.integers(2,5)), rng)
              local=local*np.exp(0.35*y)
              np.clip; normalize
              local_score=_fast_score_norm(local)
              no=0; amp=0.25
          else:
              amp=min(0.8, amp*1.002)
       if iters % 200==0 or time - last_print>5:
           print(...)
    return local, local_score
```

Let's write helper functions in detail.

`_next_pow2`:
```
def _next_pow2(m):
    L=1
    while L < m: L <<= 1
    return L
```

`_get_layout`:
```
def _get_layout(n):
    key=n
    if key in _CACHE:
        return _CACHE[key]
    m=2*n-1
    L=_next_pow2(m)
    mg=3*n-2
    LG=_next_pow2(mg)
    if n > 1:
       x=np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
       x /= float(n-1)
    else:
       x=np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)
    result=(L,LG,x)
    _CACHE[key]=result
    return result
```

`_fft_conv_norm`:
```
def _fft_conv_norm(a):
    n=int(a.size)
    if n <=0: return np.array([], dtype=float)
    L,_,_ = _get_layout(n)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, L)
    m=2*n-1
    c=c[:m]
    # Remove tiny negative roundoff.
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c
```
Potential memory: `np.maximum` allocates; ok. Could use `c[c<0]=0` if roundoff. Use `np.clip(c,0,None,out=c)`? It modifies. But if c is view? `np.fft.irfft` returns new. Use `np.clip(c,0.0,None,out=c)`.

`_fast_score_norm`:
```
def _fast_score_norm(a, return_conv=False):
    n=int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
       if return_conv: return np.inf, np.array([], dtype=float)
       return np.inf
    c=_fft_conv_norm(a)
    if c.size == 0:
       M0=0.0
    else:
       M0=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M0):
        if return_conv: return np.inf, c
        return np.inf
    score = 2.0*float(n)*M0
    if return_conv: return score, c
    return score
```

`_fast_score_generic`:
```
def _fast_score_generic(a):
    arr=np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    S=float(np.sum(arr))
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S<=1e-300:
        return np.inf
    arr /= S
    return _fast_score_norm(arr)
```

`_update_storage`:
```
def _update_storage(storage, score, arr):
    if score is None or not np.isfinite(score): return
    arr=np.asarray(arr, dtype=float)
    n=int(arr.size)
    if n==0: return
    old=storage.get(n)
    if old is None or float(score) < float(old[0]) - 1e-9:
        storage[n]=[float(score), arr.copy()]
```

`_best_from_storage`:
```
def _best_from_storage(storage):
    best_score=np.inf; best_arr=None
    for score,arr in storage.values():
       if score < best_score:
          best_score=score; best_arr=arr
    return best_arr,best_score
```

`_make_power_law`: ensure offset positive.
```
def _make_power_law(n, offset=1.0, tilt=0.0, right_power=0.0):
    if n <= 0: return np.array([],float)
    i=np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    a=np.power(np.maximum(i+float(offset), 1e-12), -0.5)
    if right_power != 0.0:
       a *= np.power(np.maximum(n - i, 1.0), float(right_power))
    if tilt != 0.0:
       if n > 1:
          x=i/float(n-1)
       else: x=np.zeros(n)
       a *= np.exp(float(tilt)*x)
    np.clip(a,0.0,None,out=a)
    S=a.sum()
    if S>0: a/=S
    return a
```
Right factor: n-i is n..1; if positive right_power, left boost. If negative, right suppress left? fine.

`_random_smooth_field`: Need layout x. If maxfreq > n maybe cap.
```
def _random_smooth_field(n, maxfreq, rng, x=None):
   if n <=0: return np.array([])
   if x is None:
      _,_,x = _get_layout(n)
   kmax=min(max(1,int(maxfreq)), max(1,n//2)) if n>1 else 0? For n=1 no field.
   y=np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
   for m in range(1,kmax+1):
      anp=rng.normal(0.0, 1.0/math.sqrt(float(m)))
      ph=rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*math.pi)
      y += anp * np.cos((2.0*math.pi*m)*x + ph)
   y -= y.mean()
   sd=float(np.std(y))
   if sd > 1e-15:
      y /= sd
   return y
```
For n small, kmax = min(maxfreq, n) maybe.

`_candidate_from_exp`:
```
def _candidate_from_exp(base, field, eta):
   cand=base*np.exp(eta*field)
   np.clip(cand,0.0,None,out=cand)
   S=cand.sum()
   if S>0: cand/=S
   else: cand=base.copy()
   return cand
```

def _make_proposal(a, rng, amp):
   n=int(a.size)
   if n==0: return a.copy()
   kind=int(rng.integers(0,3))
   if kind == 0:
      mf=int(rng.integers(2, 12))
      field=_random_smooth_field(n, mf, rng)
      eta=float(amp)*float(rng.uniform(0.2,1.2))
      cand=base? use a
   elif kind == 1:
      cand=a.copy()
      minw=max(8, n//100); maxw=max(minw+1, n//10)
      width=int(rng.integers(minw, maxw+1)) if maxw>minw else minw
      start=int(rng.integers(0, max(1,n-width+1)))
      factor=math.exp(float(rng.normal(0.0, max(0.05, amp))))
      if width>0:
         u=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,width)
         env=0.5*(1.0+np.cos(np.pi*(u-1.0)))
         mult=np.maximum(1.0+(factor-1.0)*env, 1e-12)
         cand[start:start+width] *= mult
      if rng.random() < 0.35:
         # second small block maybe at reverse location
         ...
   else:
      cand=a.copy()
      # Maybe move mass between a random source and target with envelope? Simple
      idx=int(rng.integers(n))
      frac=float(rng.uniform(0.01,0.25))
      mass= float(a[idx])*frac
      if mass>0:
         j=int(rng.integers(n))
         if j != idx:
            cand[idx] -= mass
            cand[j] += mass
   np.clip(cand,0.0,None,out=cand)
   S=float(cand.sum())
   if S>0: cand /= S
   else: cand = a.copy()
   return cand
```

if n < 32:
   kind = 2
```

minw = max(2, min(maxw, n//100 if n>=100 else 2))
if maxw < minw: minw=maxw

```

`_active_weights`:
```
def _active_weights(c, rng):
    n=c.size
    if n==0: return None
    M=float(np.max(c))
    if M<=0 or not np.isfinite(M): return None
    # Choose strategy: value margin or top fraction
    if rng.random() < 0.55:
       fracs=[0.0,0.001,0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.10,0.20]
       fr=fracs[int(rng.integers(len(fracs)))]
       thr=M*(1.0-fr)
       w=np.maximum(c-thr,0.0)
       cnt=int(np.count_nonzero(w>0))
       mincnt=max(4, n//2000)
       if cnt < mincnt:
           frac_list=[0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01]
           frac=frac_list[int(rng.integers(len(frac_list)))]
           k=max(mincnt, int(frac*n))
           k=min(n-1,k)
           part=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
           thr=float(np.min(c[part]))
           w=np.maximum(c-thr,0.0)
    else:
       frac_list=[0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005]
       frac=frac_list[int(rng.integers(len(frac_list)))]
       k=max(4, int(frac*n))
       k=min(n-1,k)
       part=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
       thr=float(np.min(c[part]))
       w=np.maximum(c-thr,0.0)
    sw=float(np.sum(w))
    if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 0.0:
       # fallback uniform on max
       w = (c == M).astype(float)
       sw=float(np.sum(w))
       if sw <=0: return None
    return w/sw
```
`k=min(n-1,k)` because argpartition requires k index < n; top k count K; if K=n, thr min all zeros. Use if K>=n: return uniform? For frac=0.5 K <n. ok.

`_active_gradient` as above. Use layout grad L:
```
def _active_gradient(w,a):
    n=int(a.size)
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    q=2*n-1
    Lg,_,_? _get_layout returns LG second
    _,Lg,_ = _get_layout(n)
    B=w[::-1]
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, Lg)
    fb=np.fft.rfft(B, Lg)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb, Lg)
    total=n+q-1
    conv=conv[:total]
    # grad = 2*conv[n-1:2n-1][::-1]
    grad=2.0*conv[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    return grad
```
Check `conv[n-1:2*n-1]` length n. For n=1: q=1, total=1? n+q-1=1. conv start 0 stop1 length1; okay. But gradient for n=1 derivative 2a0 w0; B=w0, conv=a0w0, grad 2a0w0. good.

`_gradient_candidate`:
```
def _gradient_candidate(a, rng):
   n=int(a.size)
   if n==0: return a.copy(), np.inf
   score,c=_fast_score_norm(a, True)
   w=_active_weights(c,rng)
   if w is None:
      return a.copy(), score
   grad=_active_gradient(w,a)
   if grad.size != n: return a.copy(), score
   d=grad - float(np.mean(grad))
   scale=float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
   if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale < 1e-14:
      return a.copy(), score
   d /= scale
   best_cand=a.copy(); best_s=score
   etas=[0.5,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005]
   for eta in etas:
      cand=a*np.exp(-float(eta)*d)
      np.clip(cand,0.0,None,out=cand)
      S=float(cand.sum())
      if S<=0: continue
      cand/=S
      s=_fast_score_norm(cand)
      if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_s:
         best_s=s; best_cand=cand.copy()
   return best_cand,best_s
```
Maybe if no improvement, best_s=score. Good.

`_make_basis`:
```
def _make_basis(n, K):
   if n <=1:
      return np.zeros((max(K,1),1), float)
   x=np.arange(n,dtype=float)/(n-1)
   cols=[]
   z=x-0.5
   cols.append(z)
   cols.append(z*z - (1.0/12.0))
   for m in range(1,10):
      cols.append(np.cos(2.0*math.pi*m*x))
      cols.append(np.sin(2.0*math.pi*m*x))
      if len(cols) >= K: break
   while len(cols) < K:
      cols.append(np.zeros(n,float))
   basis=np.array(cols[:K], dtype=float)
   for j in range(basis.shape[1]):
      col=basis[:,j]
      col -= col.mean()
      sd=float(np.std(col))
      if sd>1e-12:
          basis[:,j] /= sd
   return basis
```
For n small, frequency may be >n/2; still cos/sin but may alias. Fine.

`_lowdim_objective` and optimize.

`_lowdim_objective` needs data. If basis 2D, v @ basis works if v is shape (K,). Good.
Penalty: limit maybe 1.0. Additional `x.max()` pow clip if values >1000? base ~1/n; exp can at most e^{1.2*4? ~ e^4.8=120; values ~120/n <1, fine. Sum normalize.

```
def _lowdim_objective(v):
    data=_LOWDIM
    if not data: return 1e100
    v=np.asarray(v, dtype=float)
    lim=1.2
    amax=float(np.max(np.abs(v))) if v.size else 0.0
    pen=0.0
    if amax > lim:
       v=np.clip(v, -lim, lim)
       pen=0.25*(amax-lim)*(amax-lim)
    base=data['base']; basis=data['basis']
    a=base * np.exp(np.dot(v, basis))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
       return 1e100 + pen
    np.clip(a,0.0,None,out=a)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12:
       return 1e100 + pen
    a/=S
    return float(_fast_score_norm(a)) + pen
```

def _basis_search(n, rng, storage, seconds):
   start=time.monotonic()
   base=_make_power_law(n,1.0)
   basis=_make_basis(n,8)
   _LOWDIM? not needed; use direct.
   # optional scipy NM
   try import minimize
   for trial in range(2):
       v0=rng.normal(0,0.05,8)
       res minimize with callback? time.
       evaluate v0,resx, plus maybe random around.
   # random trial loop until time
   while time < start+seconds:
       v=rng.normal(0,0.15,8)
       a=base*np.exp(v@basis); normalize; score; update
```

- Maintain best v (initial 0). For many iterations, propose new v = best_v + small gaussian (or random coordinate), evaluate candidate, accept if improve. `_basis_refine(n, rng, storage, seconds)`:
```
base=_make_power_law(n,1)
basis=_make_basis(n,8)
best_v=np.zeros(8); best_score=fast(base)
local_v=best_v.copy(); local_score=best_score
amp=0.1
start=time.monotonic(); limit=start+seconds
it=0
while time<limit:
   # propose v perturb
   if rng.random()<0.8:
      new_v=local_v + rng.normal(0, amp, size=8)
   else:
      new_v=rng.normal(0,0.25, size=8)
   new_v=np.clip(new_v,-1.0,1.0)
   a=base*np.exp(new_v @ basis); normalize; s=score
   if s<local_score-1e-12:
      local_v=new_v; local_score=s; amp=max(0.02, amp*0.98)
      if s<best_score: best_v=new_v; best_score=s; update storage
   else:
      if rng.random()<0.1 and s<local_score+0.002:
         local_v=new_v; local_score=s
      amp=min(0.6, amp*1.01)
      if it%5000==0: local_v=best_v+rng.normal(0,0.1,8)
   it+=1
```
This refines within basis. Good and top-level.

It uses v@basis O(8n) plus FFT per iteration; similar to full random but lower-dim. We can run for each selected length 30s? 30s ~ 4000 evals. Good.

Implement `_basis_refine` top-level. It needs storage and update. It can also update storage with best.

```
def _basis_refine(n, rng, storage, seconds, init_v=None):
    if n<=0 or seconds<=0: return
    base=_make_power_law(n,1.0)
    basis=_make_basis(n,8)
    # Ensure base score stored
    s0=_fast_score_norm(base)
    _update_storage(storage,s0,base)
    local_v = np.zeros(basis.shape[1]) if init_v is None else np.asarray(init_v)
    local_score=s0
    best_v=local_v.copy()
    best_score=local_score
    amp=0.12
    limit=time.monotonic()+seconds
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()<limit:
       it+=1
       if rng.random()<0.85:
          local_v_new = local_v + rng.normal(0.0, amp, size=basis.shape[1])
       else:
          local_v_new = rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, size=basis.shape[1])
       local_v_new=np.clip(local_v_new, -1.0, 1.0)
       a=base*np.exp(local_v_new @ basis)
       np.clip(a,0,None,out=a); S=a.sum(); if S>0: a/=S
       s=_fast_score_norm(a)
       if np.isfinite(s) and s < local_score - 1e-12:
          local_v=local_v_new; local_score=s; amp=max(0.02, amp*0.98)
          if s<best_score:
             best_score=s; best_v=local_v.copy(); arr=a.copy(); _update_storage(storage,s,arr); local_score=s
       else:
          if np.isfinite(s) and s < local_score + 0.003 and rng.random()<0.15:
             local_v=local_v_new; local_score=s
          if it%2000==0:
             # reheat local_v
             local_v = best_v + rng.normal(0.0,0.08, size=basis.shape[1])
             local_v=np.clip(local_v,-1,1)
             a=base*np.exp(local_v@basis); normalize; local_score=_fast_score_norm(a)
          amp=min(0.5, amp*1.01)
    # final ensure best stored maybe if local best
    # Evaluate best_v
    arr=base*np.exp(best_v@basis); normalize; s=fast; update
```
But `local_v @ basis` works if local_v 1D and basis shape (K,n) returns 1D? In numpy, (K,) @ (K,n) => (n,), yes. Good.
Need if basis K could be >? K=8. If n small, basis columns maybe zero; fine.
Potential bug: after reheat local_v, if local_v is view? no.
Need store array when best_score updated; if not, final.

   else base=base/n? ensure sum1.
```

`_local_search` uses `storage` and `best_ref`? It updates storage when local improves. It also can reset local from storage items.

```
def _local_search(storage, rng, hard_deadline):
    best_arr,best_score=_best_from_storage(storage)
    if best_arr is None: return
    local=best_arr.copy(); local_score=best_score
    amp=0.15
    no=0
    it=0
    last_print=time.monotonic()
    while time.monotonic() < hard_deadline:
       it+=1
       # exit if hard deadline
       rolled=rng.random()
       if rolled < 0.2:
          cand,s=_gradient_candidate(local,rng)
       else:
          cand=_make_proposal(local,rng,amp)
          s=_fast_score_norm(cand)
       if not np.isfinite(s): cand=local; s=local_score
       if s < local_score - 1e-12:
          local=cand; local_score=s; no=0
          if s < best_score: best_score=s; _update_storage(storage,s,cand)
          amp=max(0.04, amp*0.99)
       else:
          no += 1
          if s < local_score + 0.003 and rng.random()<0.2:
             local=cand; local_score=s
          if no > 5000:
             # reheat from global best
             b_arr,b_score=_best_from_storage(storage)
             if b_arr is not None:
                y=_random_smooth_field(local.size, int(rng.integers(2,8)), rng)
                local=b_arr*np.exp(0.25*y); normalize; local_score=_fast_score_norm(local)
                no=0; amp=0.25
                # optionally choose another stored length every 5times
             # sometimes reset to a different length
             if no==0 and len(storage)>1 and rng.random()<0.3:
                # choose another
                others=[(sc,a) for (sc,a) in storage.values() if a.size != local.size]
                if others:
                   sc,a=others[int(rng.integers(len(others)))]
                   local=a.copy(); local_score=sc
       amp = min(0.8, amp*1.003) if no>0 else max(0.04, amp*0.99)
       if it%200==0 or time.monotonic()-last_print>5:
          b_arr,b_score=_best_from_storage(storage)
          print(...)
    # final update
    _update_storage(storage, local_score, local)
```

   # reset to a stored candidate (prefer global best or random)

   if items:
      if len(items)==1 or rng.random()<0.7:
         # global best
         b_arr,b_score=_best_from_storage(storage)
         local=b_arr.copy()
      else:
         rand_item = items[int(rng.integers(len(items)))]

      # optional mutation

         y=_random_smooth_field(local.size, int(rng.integers(2,6)), rng)
         local=local*np.exp(0.2*y); normalize; local_score=_fast_score_norm(local)
      else:
         local_score=_fast_score_norm(local)
      no=0; amp=0.2
```

```
def _to_list(arr):
   arr=np.asarray(arr,float)
   arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
   S=float(arr.sum())
   if S>0: arr=arr/S
   return [float(x) for x in arr]
```

Want symmetric peak center: use `0.5*(1+np.cos(np.pi*(2*u-1)))`. At u=0: cos(-π)=-1=>0; u=0.5 cos0=1=>1; u=1 cosπ=-1=>0. So env =0.5*(1+cos(π*(2u-1))). Our formula `u-1`: at u=0 cos(-π)=-1 ->0; u=0.5 cos(-0.5π)=0 ->0.5; u=1 cos0=1 ->1. That's peak at end, not center. Correct to:
```
env=0.5*(1.0 + np.cos(np.pi*(2.0*u - 1.0)))
```

def _basis_refine(n, rng, storage, seconds, base=None):
    if n <= 0 or seconds <= 0:
        return
    if base is None:
        base = _make_power_law(n, 1.0, 0.0, 0.0)
    base = np.asarray(base, dtype=float)
    base = np.clip(base, 0.0, None)
    Sb = float(base.sum())
    if Sb <= 0.0:
        base = _make_power_law(n, 1.0, 0.0, 0.0)
        Sb = ...
    base = base / Sb
    basis = _make_basis(n, 8)
    K = basis.shape[1]
    best_v = np.zeros(K, dtype=float)
    best_score = _fast_score_norm(base)
    _update_storage(storage, best_score, base)
    local_v = best_v.copy()
    local_score = best_score
    amp = 0.10
    limit = time.monotonic() + float(seconds)
    it = 0
    while time.monotonic() < limit:
       it += 1
       if rng.random() < 0.8:
           local_v = local_v + rng.normal(0.0, amp, size=K)
       else:
           local_v = rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, size=K)
       local_v = np.clip(local_v, -1.5, 1.5)
       a = base * np.exp(np.dot(local_v, basis))
       np.clip(a, 0.0, None, out=a)
       S = float(a.sum())
       if S <= 0.0:
           continue
       a /= S
       s = _fast_score_norm(a)
       if np.isfinite(s):
           if s < local_score - 1e-12:
               local_score = s
               amp = max(0.02, amp*0.985)
               if s < best_score - 1e-12:
                   best_score = s
                   best_v = local_v.copy()
                   _update_storage(storage, best_score, a.copy())
           else:
               if s < local_score + 0.003 and rng.random() < 0.2:
                   local_score = s
               if it % 3000 == 0:
                   local_v = best_v + rng.normal(0.0, 0.08, size=K)
                   local_v = np.clip(local_v, -1.5, 1.5)
                   a2 = base * np.exp(np.dot(local_v, basis)); clip; S; if S>0 a2/=S
                   local_score = _fast_score_norm(a2)
               amp = min(0.6, amp*1.005)
    # final ensure best stored by recomputing from best_v
    a = base * np.exp(np.dot(best_v, basis)); normalize; s=fast; update

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative coefficients minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a))^2.

Strategy:
- Use fast FFT-based self-convolution as the objective in the inner loop.
- Start from sparse initial guesses, especially a shifted 1/sqrt(i) law, which is
  close to the large-n continuous optimum.
- Refine inside a low-dimensional smooth exponent space (Fourier/polynomial basis)
  to obtain a good non-uniform profile.
- Then run a long local/search phase with:
    * smooth random multiplicative perturbations,
    * random block perturbations,
    * mass-transfer coordinate moves,
    * subgradient steps using active (near-maximal) convolution coefficients.
- Keep the best candidate per length and evaluate the final saved candidates rigorously
  with evaluate_sequence().
"""

import numpy as np
import math
import time

_CACHE = {}


def _next_pow2(m):
    L = 1
    while L < m:
        L <<= 1
    return L


def _get_layout(n):
    key = int(n)
    if key in _CACHE:
        return _CACHE[key]
    m = 2 * key - 1
    Lconv = _next_pow2(m)
    mgrad = 3 * key - 2
    Lgrad = _next_pow2(mgrad)
    if key > 1:
        x = np.arange(key, dtype=np.float64)
        x /= float(key - 1)
    else:
        x = np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)
    res = (Lconv, Lgrad, x)
    _CACHE[key] = res
    return res


def _fft_conv_norm(a):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    L, _, _ = _get_layout(n)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)
    c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    np.clip(c, 0.0, None, out=c)
    return c


def _fast_score_norm(a, return_conv=False):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        if return_conv:
            return float("inf"), np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
        return float("inf")
    c = _fft_conv_norm(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        M = 0.0
    else:
        M = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M):
        if return_conv:
            return float("inf"), c
        return float("inf")
    score = 2.0 * float(n) * M
    if return_conv:
        return score, c
    return score


def _fast_score_generic(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(np.sum(arr))
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    arr /= S
    return _fast_score_norm(arr)


def _update_storage(storage, score, arr):
    if score is None or not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(arr.size)
    if n == 0:
        return
    old = storage.get(n)
    if old is None or float(score) < float(old[0]) - 1e-10:
        storage[n] = [float(score), arr.copy()]


def _best_from_storage(storage):
    best_score = float("inf")
    best_arr = None
    for item in storage.values():
        if item[0] < best_score:
            best_score = item[0]
            best_arr = item[1]
    return best_arr, best_score


def _make_
power_law(n, offset=1.0, tilt=0.0, right_power=0.0):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.power(np.maximum(i + float(offset), 1e-12), -0.5)
    if right_power != 0.0:
        a *= np.power(np.maximum(float(n) - i, 1.0), float(right_power))
    if tilt != 0.0:
        if n > 1:
            x = i / float(n - 1)
        else:
            x = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        a *= np.exp(float(tilt) * x)
    np.clip(a, 0.0, None, out=a)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S > 0.0:
        a /= S
    return a


def _random_smooth_field(n, maxfreq, rng, x=None):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if x is None:
        _, _, x = _get_layout(n)
    kmax = min(max(1, int(maxfreq)), max(1, n // 2) if n > 1 else 0)
    y = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, kmax + 1):
        amp = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0 / math.sqrt(float(m)))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        y += amp * np.cos((2.0 * math.pi * float(m)) * x + ph)
    if n > 0:
        y -= float(y.mean())
    sd = float(np.std(y))
    if sd > 1e-15:
        y /= sd
    return y


def _make_proposal(a, rng, amp):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return a.copy()

    kind = int(rng.integers(0, 3))

    if kind == 0:
        # Smooth global log-perturbation.
        mf = int(rng.integers(2, 13))
        field = _random_smooth_field(n, mf, rng)
        eta = float(amp) * float(rng.uniform(0.2, 1.2))
        cand = a * np.exp(eta * field)
    elif kind == 1:
        # Cosine-in-window multiplicative move.
        cand = a.copy()
        if n <= 16:
            kind2 = 2
        else:
            kind2 = 1

        if kind2 == 1:
            maxw = max(2, min(n, n // 10 if n >= 20 else n))
            minw = max(2, min(maxw, n // 100 if n >= 100 else 2))
            if maxw < minw:
                minw = maxw
            width = int(rng.integers(minw, maxw + 1))
            if width > 0:
                start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
                factor = math.exp(float(rng.normal(0.0, max(0.05, float(amp)))))
                u = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, width)
                env = 0.5 * (1.0 + np.cos(math.pi * (2.0 * u - 1.0)))
                mult = 1.0 + (factor - 1.0) * env
                np.clip(mult, 1e-12, None, out=mult)
                cand[start:start + width] *= mult

            if rng.random() < 0.35:
                width2 = int(rng.integers(max(2, n // 200), max(3, n // 40) + 1))
                if width2 > 0 and width2 <= n:
                    start2 = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width2 + 1)))
                    factor2 = math.exp(float(rng.normal(0.0, max(0.03, float(amp) * 0.7))))
                    u2 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, width2)
                    env2 = 0.5 * (1.0 + np.cos(math.pi * (2.0 * u2 - 1.0)))
                    mult2 = 1.0 + (factor2 - 1.0) * env2
                    np.clip(mult2, 1e-12, None, out=mult2)
                    cand[start2:start2 + width2] *= mult2
    else:
        # Coordinate mass transfer.
        cand = a.copy()
        idx = int(rng.integers(n))
        frac = float(rng.uniform(0.01, 0.25))
        mass = float(a[idx]) * frac
        if mass > 0.0:
            j = int(rng.integers(n))
            if j != idx:
                cand[idx] -= mass
                cand[j] += mass

    np.clip(cand, 0.0, None, out=cand)
    S = float(cand.sum())
    if S > 0.0:
        cand /= S
    else:
        cand = a.copy()
    return cand


def _active_weights(c, rng):
    n = int(c.size)
    if n == 0:
        return None
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return None

    if rng.random() < 0.55:
        fracs = [0.0, 0.001, 0.002, 0.005, 0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20]
        fr = fracs[int(rng.integers(len(fracs)))]
        thr = M * (1.0 - fr)
        w = np.maximum(c - thr, 0.0)
        cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(w > 0.0))
        mincnt = max(2, min(n - 1, n // 1000))
        if cnt < mincnt:
            frac_list = [0.50, 0.25, 0.10, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01]
            frac = frac_list[int(rng.integers(len(frac_list)))]
            k = max(mincnt, int(round(frac * float(n))))
            k = min(n - 1, max(1, k))
            part = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
            thr = float(np.min(c[part]))
            w = np.maximum(c - thr, 0.0)
    else:
        frac_list = [0.50, 0.25, 0.10, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005]
        frac = frac_list[int(rng.integers(len(frac_list)))]
        k = max(2, int(round(frac * float(n))))
        k = min(n - 1, max(1, k))
        part = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
        thr = float(np.min(c[part]))
        w = np.maximum(c - thr, 0.0)

    sw = float(np.sum(w))
    if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 0.0:
        w = (c == M).astype(np.float64)
        sw = float(np.sum(w))
        if sw <= 0.0:
            return None
    return w / sw


def _active_gradient(w, a):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    _, Lg, _ = _get_layout(n)
    q = 2 * n - 1
    B = w[::-1]
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, Lg)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(B, Lg)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, Lg)
    total = n + q - 1
    conv = conv[:total]
    # index for r = 0..n-1 is (q-1)-r = 2n-2-r
    grad = 2.0 * conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
    return grad


def _gradient_candidate(a, rng):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return a.copy(), float("inf")
    score, c = _fast_score_norm(a, return_conv=True)
    w = _active_weights(c, rng)
    if w is None:
        return a.copy(), score
    grad = _active_gradient(w, a)
    if grad.size != n:
        return a.copy(), score

    d = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
    scale = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
    if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale < 1e-14:
        return a.copy(), score
    d /= scale

    best_cand = a.copy()
    best_s = score
    etas = [0.5, 0.2, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005]
    for eta in etas:
        cand = a * np.exp(-float(eta) * d)
        np.clip(cand, 0.0, None, out=cand)
        S = float(cand.sum())
        if S <= 0.0:
            continue
        cand /= S
        s = _fast_score_norm(cand)
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_s:
            best_s = s
            best_cand = cand.copy()
    return best_cand, best_s


def _make_basis(n, K):
    K = max(1, int(K))
    if n <= 1:
        return np.zeros((K, max(1, n)), dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n - 1)
    z = x - 0.5
    cols = []
    cols.append(z)
    cols.append(z * z - (1.0 / 12.0))
    m = 1
    while len(cols) < K:
        cols.append(np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * float(m) * x))
        if len(cols) < K:
            cols.append(np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * float(m) * x))
        m += 1
    while len(cols) < K:
        cols.append(np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64))
    basis = np.array(cols[:K], dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(basis.shape[1]):
        col = basis[:, j]
        col -= float(col.mean())
        sd = float(col.std())
        if sd > 1e-12:
            basis[:, j] /= sd
    return basis


def _basis_refine(n, rng, storage, seconds, base=None):
    if n <= 0 or seconds <= 0:
        return

    if base is None:
        base = _make_power_law(n, 1.0, 0.0, 0.0)
    base = np.asarray(base, dtype=np.float64)
    np.clip(base, 0.0, None, out=base)
    Sb = float(base.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(Sb) or Sb <= 1e-300:
        base = _make_power_law(n, 1.0, 0.0, 0.0)
        Sb = float(base.sum())
    if Sb <= 1e-300:
        return
    base /= Sb

    basis = _make_basis(n, 8)
    K = basis.shape[1]

    best_v = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
    best_score = _fast_score_norm(base)
    _update_storage(storage, best_score, base)

    local_v = best_v.copy()
    local_score = best_score
    amp = 0.12
    limit = time.monotonic() + float(seconds)
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < limit:
        it += 1
        if rng.random() < 0.82:
            new_v = local_v + rng.normal(0.0, amp, size=K)
        else:
            new_v = rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, size=K)
        new_v = np.clip(new_v, -1.5, 1.5)

        a = base * np.exp(np.dot(new_v, basis))
        np.clip(a, 0.0, None, out=a)
        S = float(a.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 1e-300:
            continue
        a /= S
        s = _fast_score_norm(a)

        if not np.isfinite(s):
            continue

        if s < local_score - 1e-12:
            local_v = new_v
            local_score = s
            amp = max(0.025, amp * 0.985)
            if s < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = s
                best_v = new_v.copy()
                _update_storage(storage, best_score, a.copy())
        else:
            if s < local_score + 0.003 and rng.random() < 0.25:
                local_v = new_v
                local_score = s
            if it % 3000 == 0:
                local_v = best_v + rng.normal(0.0, 0.08, size=K)
                local_v = np.clip(local_v, -1.5, 1.5)
                a2 = base * np.exp(np.dot(local_v, basis))
                np.clip(a2, 0.0, None, out=a2)
                S2 = float(a2.sum())
                if S2 > 0.0:
                    a2 /= S2
                    local_score = _fast_score_norm(a2)
            amp = min(0.6, amp * 1.004)

    # Make sure the best point found in this low-dimensional manifold is saved.
    a = base * np.exp(np.dot(best_v, basis))
    np.clip(a, 0.0, None, out=a)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S > 0.0:
        a /= S
        s = _fast_score_norm(a)
        if s < best_score - 1e-12:
            _update_storage(storage, s, a.copy())


def _local_search(storage, rng, deadline):
    best_arr, best_score = _best_from_storage(storage)
    if best_arr is None:
        return

    local = np.asarray(best_arr, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    local_score = _fast_score_norm(local)
    _update_storage(storage, local_score, local)

    amp = 0.16
    stale = 0
    iters = 0
    last_print = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        iters += 1
        r = float(rng.random())

        if r < 0.18:
            cand, s = _gradient_candidate(local, rng)
        else:
            cand = _make_proposal(local, rng, amp)
            s = _fast_score_norm(cand)

        if not np.isfinite(s):
            cand = local
            s = local_score

        if s < local_score - 1e-12:
            local = cand
            local_score = s
            stale = 0
            amp = max(0.035, amp * 0.988)
            _update_storage(storage, local_score, local)
        else:
            stale += 1
            if s < local_score + 0.004 and rng.random() < 0.2:
                local = cand
                local_score = s
                _update_storage(storage, local_score, local)

            if stale > 6000:
                items = list(storage.values())
                if items:
                    if len(items) > 1 and rng.random() < 0.25:
                        item = items[int(rng.integers(len(items)))]
                    else:
                        best_arr, best_score = _best_from_storage(storage)
                        item = [best_score, best_arr]
                    if item is not None and item[1] is not None:
                        local = np.asarray(item[1], dtype=np.float64).copy()
                        local_score = float(item[0])
                        maybe_mutate = float(rng.random())
                        if maybe_mutate < 0.55 and local.size > 0:
                            mf = int(rng.integers(2, 7))
                            y = _random_smooth_field(local.size, mf, rng)
                            local = local * np.exp(0.25 * y)
                            np.clip(local, 0.0, None, out=local)
                            S = float(local.sum())
                            if S > 0.0:
                                local /= S
                                local_score = _fast_score_norm(local)
                        elif maybe_mutate < 0.75 and local.size > 0:
                            # New basis-based random restart at current length.
                            base = _make_power_law(local.size, 1.0, 0.0, 0.0)
                            basis = _make_basis(local.size, 8)
                            v = rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, size=basis.shape[1])
                            local = base * np.exp(np.dot(v, basis))
                            np.clip(local, 0.0, None, out=local)
                            S = float(local.sum())
                            if S > 0.0:
                                local /= S
                                local_score = _fast_score_norm(local)
                        _update_storage(storage, local_score, local)
                        stale = 0
                        amp = 0.25
                    else:
                        stale = 0
                amp = min(0.85, amp * 1.005)

        if iters % 200 == 0 or (time.monotonic() - last_print) > 5.0:
            best_arr, best_score = _best_from_storage(storage)
            print(
                "t={:.1f} best_fast={:.8f} local_fast={:.8f} len={} stale={} iters={}"
                .format(
                    time.monotonic() - 0.0,
                    best_score if np.isfinite(best_score) else -1.0,
                    local_score if np.isfinite(local_score) else -1.0,
                    int(local.size),
                    stale,
                    iters
                ),
                flush=True
            )
            last_print = time.monotonic()

    _update_storage(storage, local_score, local)


def _tolist(arr):
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(arr.sum())
    if S > 0.0:
        arr = arr / S
    out = [float(x) for x in arr]
    # Ensure at least one positive number.
    if not out:
        out = [1.0]
    for i in range(len(out)):
        if out[i] > 0.0:
            return out
    out[0] = 1.0
    return out


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """Return a nonnegative coefficient sequence with low evaluate_sequence() score."""
    try:
        seed_value = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        seed_value = 42
    try:
        budget_value = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget_value = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget_value) or budget_value <= 10.0:
        return [1.0]

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    hard_deadline = t0 + max(20.0, budget_value - 8.0)
    print("start budget={}s hard_deadline_in={:.1f}s".format(budget_value, hard_deadline - t0), flush=True)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed_value)
    storage = {}

    # Candidate lengths: prioritize the known construction length, then good FFT-friendly lengths.
    priority_lengths = []
    try:
        hs = height_sequence_1
        hs_arr = np.asarray(hs, dtype=np.float64)
        hs_arr = np.clip(hs_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        S = float(hs_arr.sum())
        if hs_arr.size > 0 and np.isfinite(S) and S > 1e-300 and 128 <= hs_arr.size <= 12000:
            hs_arr /= S
            hs_score = _fast_score_norm(hs_arr)
            if np.isfinite(hs_score):
                _update_storage(storage, hs_score, hs_arr)
                if hs_arr.size not in priority_lengths:
                    priority_lengths.append(hs_arr.size)
            print("height_sequence_1 fast={:.8f} len={}".format(hs_score, hs_arr.size), flush=True)
    except Exception:
        pass

    for n in (5582, 4096, 8192, 9216):
        if 128 <= n <= 12000 and n not in priority_lengths:
            priority_lengths.append(n)

    if not priority_lengths:
        priority_lengths = [4096]

    init_budget = max(25.0, min(180.0, 0.07 * budget_value))
    init_deadline = min(hard_deadline - 90.0, t0 + init_budget)

    # Initial deterministic-ish scans of simple asymmetric power laws.
    initial_done_any = False
    for n in priority_lengths:
        if time.monotonic() > init_deadline and initial_done_any:
            break
        best_s = float("inf")
        best_arr = None
        for offset in (1.0, 1.75, 3.0, 6.0):
            for tilt in (-0.12, 0.0, 0.12):
                for rpw in (-0.10, 0.0, 0.10):
                    if time.monotonic() > init_deadline:
                        break
                    arr = _make_power_law(n, offset, tilt, rpw)
                    s = _fast_score_norm(arr)
                    if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_s:
                        best_s = s
                        best_arr = arr.copy()
        if best_arr is not None:
            _update_storage(storage, best_s, best_arr)
            initial_done_any = True

    if not storage:
        arr = _make_power_law(4096, 1.0, 0.0, 0.0)
        storage[4096] = [float(_fast_score_norm(arr)), arr.copy()]

    # Low-dimensional smooth refinement on the currently best candidate.
    best_arr, best_score = _best_from_storage(storage)
    if best_arr is not None:
        basis_budget = max(15.0, min(240.0, 0.10 * budget_value))
        basis_deadline = min(hard_deadline - 90.0, time.monotonic() + basis_budget)
        if basis_deadline > time.monotonic() + 0.5:
            _basis_refine(
                int(best_arr.size),
                rng,
                storage,
                max(0.0, basis_deadline - time.monotonic()),
                base=best_arr
            )

    # Optionally perform another low-dimensional refine on a different stored length, if time allows.
    if budget_value > 1200.0:
        best_arr, best_score = _best_from_storage(storage)
        if best_arr is not None and len(storage) > 1:
            other = None
            for item in storage.values():
                if item[1] is not None and item[1].size != best_arr.size:
                    if other is None or item[0] < other[0]:
                        other = item
            if other is not None:
                extra_budget = max(10.0, min(90.0, 0.04 * budget_value))
                extra_deadline = min(hard_deadline - 90.0, time.monotonic() + extra_budget)
                if extra_deadline > time.monotonic() + 0.5:
                    _basis_refine(
                        int(other[1].size),
                        rng,
                        storage,
                        max(0.0, extra_deadline - time.monotonic()),
                        base=other[1]
                    )

    # Main local search over coefficients.
    local_deadline = hard_deadline - 90.0
    if local_deadline > time.monotonic() + 1.0:
        _local_search(storage, rng, local_deadline)

    # Final rigorous evaluation of the stored best candidates.
    best_fast_arr, best_fast_score = _best_from_storage(storage)
    if best_fast_arr is None:
        return [1.0]

    best_fast_list = _tolist(best_fast_arr)
    best_exact_list = best_fast_list
    best_exact_score = float('inf')

    # Evaluate all stored saved sequences. Usually only a few.
    for item in storage.values():
        if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 2.0:
            break
        arr = item[1]
        if arr is None:
            continue
        lst = _tolist(arr)
        try:
            exact = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
        except Exception:
            exact = float('inf')
        if np.isfinite(exact) and exact < best_exact_score:
            best_exact_score = exact
            best_exact_list = lst
            print("exact verified score={:.8f} len={}".format(exact, len(lst)), flush=True)

    print(
        "final best_fast={:.8f} best_exact_known={:.8f} len={}"
        .format(
            best_fast_score,
            best_exact_score if np.isfinite(best_exact_score) else -1.0,
            len(best_exact_list)
        ),
        flush=True
    )

    return best_exact_list
```
